SELECT [b].[Id], [b].[CreatedAt], [b].[Description], [b].[IsActive], [b].[NoOfPages], [b].[PdfFilePath], [b].[Title], [b].[UpdatedAt], [s].[BookId], [s].[LanguageId], [s].[Id], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[Description], [s].[IsActive], [s].[Name], [s].[UpdatedAt], [s0].[BookId], [s0].[ColorId], [s0].[Id], [s0].[CreatedAt], [s0].[IsActive], [s0].[Name], [s0].[UpdatedAt], [s1].[Id], [s1].[BookId], [s1].[CreatedAt], [s1].[CurrencyId], [s1].[UpdatedAt], [s1].[amount], [s1].[id0], [s1].[CreatedAt0], [s1].[IsActive], [s1].[Title], [s1].[UpdatedAt0], [s1].[description]
FROM [Books] AS [b]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [b0].[BookId], [b0].[LanguageId], [l].[Id], [l].[CreatedAt], [l].[Description], [l].[IsActive], [l].[Name], [l].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [BookLanguages] AS [b0]
    INNER JOIN [Languages] AS [l] ON [b0].[LanguageId] = [l].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [b].[Id] = [s].[BookId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [b1].[BookId], [b1].[ColorId], [c].[Id], [c].[CreatedAt], [c].[IsActive], [c].[Name], [c].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [BookColors] AS [b1]
    INNER JOIN [Colors] AS [c] ON [b1].[ColorId] = [c].[Id]
) AS [s0] ON [b].[Id] = [s0].[BookId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [b2].[Id], [b2].[BookId], [b2].[CreatedAt], [b2].[CurrencyId], [b2].[UpdatedAt], [b2].[amount], [c0].[id] AS [id0], [c0].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt0], [c0].[IsActive], [c0].[Title], [c0].[UpdatedAt] AS [UpdatedAt0], [c0].[description]
    FROM [BookPrices] AS [b2]
    INNER JOIN [Currency] AS [c0] ON [b2].[CurrencyId] = [c0].[id]

    SELECT [b1].[BookId], [b1].[ColorId], [c].[Id], [c].[CreatedAt], [c].[IsActive], [c].[Name], [c].[UpdatedAt]

SELECT [b].[Id], [b].[CreatedAt], [b].[Description], [b].[IsActive], [b].[NoOfPages], [b].[PdfFilePath], [b].[Title], [b].[UpdatedAt], [s].[BookId], [s].[LanguageId], [s].[Id], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[Description], [s].[IsActive], [s].[Name], [s].[UpdatedAt], [s0].[BookId], [s0].[ColorId], [s0].[Id], [s0].[CreatedAt], [s0].[IsActive], [s0].[Name], [s0].[UpdatedAt], [s1].[Id], [s1].[BookId], [s1].[CreatedAt], [s1].[CurrencyId], [s1].[UpdatedAt], [s1].[amount], [s1].[id0], [s1].[CreatedAt0], [s1].[IsActive], [s1].[Title], [s1].[UpdatedAt0], [s1].[description]
FROM [Books] AS [b]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [b0].[BookId], [b0].[LanguageId], [l].[Id], [l].[CreatedAt], [l].[Description], [l].[IsActive], [l].[Name], [l].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [BookLanguages] AS [b0]
    INNER JOIN [Languages] AS [l] ON [b0].[LanguageId] = [l].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [b].[Id] = [s].[BookId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [b1].[BookId], [b1].[ColorId], [c].[Id], [c].[CreatedAt], [c].[IsActive], [c].[Name], [c].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [BookColors] AS [b1]
    INNER JOIN [Colors] AS [c] ON [b1].[ColorId] = [c].[Id]
) AS [s0] ON [b].[Id] = [s0].[BookId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [b2].[Id], [b2].[BookId], [b2].[CreatedAt], [b2].[CurrencyId], [b2].[UpdatedAt], [b2].[amount], [c0].[id] AS [id0], [c0].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt0], [c0].[IsActive], [c0].[Title], [c0].[UpdatedAt] AS [UpdatedAt0], [c0].[description]
    FROM [BookPrices] AS [b2]
    INNER JOIN [Currency] AS [c0] ON [b2].[CurrencyId] = [c0].[id]
) AS [s1] ON [b].[Id] = [s1].[BookId]
ORDER BY [b].[Id], [s].[BookId], [s].[LanguageId], [s].[Id], [s0].[BookId], [s0].[ColorId], [s0].[Id], [s1].[Id]

SELECT [b].[Id], [b].[CreatedAt], [b].[Description], [b].[IsActive], [b].[NoOfPages], [b].[PdfFilePath], [b].[Title], [b].[UpdatedAt], [s].[BookId], [s].[LanguageId], [s].[Id], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[Description], [s].[IsActive], [s].[Name], [s].[UpdatedAt], [s0].[BookId], [s0].[ColorId], [s0].[Id], [s0].[CreatedAt], [s0].[IsActive], [s0].[Name], [s0].[UpdatedAt], [s1].[Id], [s1].[BookId], [s1].[CreatedAt], [s1].[CurrencyId], [s1].[UpdatedAt], [s1].[amount], [s1].[id0], [s1].[CreatedAt0], [s1].[IsActive], [s1].[Title], [s1].[UpdatedAt0], [s1].[description]
FROM [Books] AS [b]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [b0].[BookId], [b0].[LanguageId], [l].[Id], [l].[CreatedAt], [l].[Description], [l].[IsActive], [l].[Name], [l].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [BookLanguages] AS [b0]
    INNER JOIN [Languages] AS [l] ON [b0].[LanguageId] = [l].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [b].[Id] = [s].[BookId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [b1].[BookId], [b1].[ColorId], [c].[Id], [c].[CreatedAt], [c].[IsActive], [c].[Name], [c].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [BookColors] AS [b1]
    INNER JOIN [Colors] AS [c] ON [b1].[ColorId] = [c].[Id]
) AS [s0] ON [b].[Id] = [s0].[BookId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [b2].[Id], [b2].[BookId], [b2].[CreatedAt], [b2].[CurrencyId], [b2].[UpdatedAt], [b2].[amount], [c0].[id] AS [id0], [c0].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt0], [c0].[IsActive], [c0].[Title], [c0].[UpdatedAt] AS [UpdatedAt0], [c0].[description]
    FROM [BookPrices] AS [b2]
    INNER JOIN [Currency] AS [c0] ON [b2].[CurrencyId] = [c0].[id]
) AS [s1] ON [b].[Id] = [s1].[BookId]
ORDER BY [b].[Id], [s].[BookId], [s].[LanguageId], [s].[Id], [s0].[BookId], [s0].[ColorId], [s0].[Id], [s1].[Id]
